# REGISTRY_INDEX — MEET-THM registrar index (what already exists, traced to source)

Registrar run for the theorem-selection meeting (MEET-THM). **Mechanical index only: no proving, no judging, no status upgrade.** Every status word below is copied verbatim from the cited source; every line carries a source path in the form `origin/<branch>:<path>[:Lnn]`. Branch `theory/MEET-THM-REG`, created from `origin/theory/campaign`. Generated by `theory_campaign/MEET-THM/evidence/registry_index_scripts/build_registry_index.py` (reads the sources from git objects; re-runnable).

**Status vocabulary:** `theory_campaign/PROTOCOL.md` (PROVED, PROOF SKETCH, COUNTEREXAMPLE, CONJECTURE, OPEN; every PROVED is a candidate: not reviewed by adoption, not adopted). Registry words PROVED-UNDER, REFEREE, PROVED-LOCKED are copied as the sources use them.

**Conventions used in the tables.**
- *Same-family (SF)* and *cross-family (XF)* are the registry's own columns (`REGISTRY_v2.md` header: SF = a campaign verifier run, Opus checking Opus; XF = A-REV1–A-REV9, Astra reviewers; A-REV4 on A-DRAFT is Astra-on-Astra and is recorded as SF there). Source: `origin/theory/T38-RECORD:theory_campaign/T38-RECORD/REGISTRY_v2.md:L7` ('How to read the columns').
- **Flag** is derived by rule from the SF/XF cells and is only a flag, not a verdict: `XF-VERIFIED` = a cross-family reviewer wrote VERIFIED (`*` = with a stated qualification); `XF-ADVERSE:GAP|ERROR` = a cross-family reviewer wrote GAP/ERROR on that row; `FLAG SAME-FAMILY-ONLY` = the only clean check is a same-model-family verifier; `FLAG AUTHOR-ONLY` = no verifier of any family (author's own check, or author = verifier); `ASTRA-ONLY` = A-DRAFT results written and checked by Astra only. For non-registry results the flag is set from the check history the source states (family of author/checker given where the source names it: Opus 5.5, Fable 5.1 = Claude family; gpt-6-astra / Astra / Sol = OpenAI family).
- **Result lives at**: `origin/theory/<BRANCH>:theory_campaign/<BRANCH>/<file>` resolved from the registry "Source" cell and checked to exist on that branch; the text after `·` is the registry's own section/label. 
- **Method variant**: filled only where a source states it (keyword scan of the registry row's own cells for LS0 / ideal vs coded grid / equal-weight vs Ẑ-weighted / v3.1, v3 as run, v3.2; quoted with the cell it came from), or an item-level statement in the draft list / revision inputs (quoted and cited). Otherwise `not stated`.
- The full verbatim SF / XF / repair / premise cells of every registry row cited here are in **Appendix A** (with `REGISTRY_v2.md` line numbers).
